\displaystyle(\mathcal{E}^{0,q}_{k}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{0,q}_{z}(t))^{1/q}

\displaystyle\leq(\mathcal{E}^{0,q}_{k}(0)+\mathcal{E}^{0,q}_{z}(0))^{1/q}e^{10\max(1,\alpha)[1+\frac{1}{2}A\varepsilon]t}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{\frac{2}{\min(1,\alpha)}}(2+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})\max(\|k^{\prime}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},\|z^{\prime}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})e^{20(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})[1+\frac{1}{2}A\varepsilon]}

for all
t\in[0,T]
. If
\varepsilon
is small enough that
20(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})(1+B_{z}\varepsilon)<21
, sending
q\rightarrow\infty
now gives us the bound
|\mathring{Z}|\leq B_{z}\max(\|k^{\prime}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},\|z^{\prime}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})
in the same manner as before, which finishes our bootstrap argument.
∎

Proposition 4.2
.

If
\varepsilon
is chosen small enough so that

(1+B_{z})^{2}\varepsilon\ll 1

then the blowup time
T_{*}
satisfies

(4.12a)

(1-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\tfrac{2}{1+\alpha}<T_{*}<(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\tfrac{2}{1+\alpha},

and in particular

(4.12b)

T_{*}=\tfrac{2}{1+\alpha}[1+\mathcal{O}((1+B_{z})\varepsilon)].

It follows immediately from (
4.12a
) that (
4.7
) holds for all
(x,t)\in\mathbb{T}\times[0,T_{*})
.

Proof.

Recall from the proof of the previous proposition that

\displaystyle\eta_{x}\mathring{W}

\displaystyle=w^{\prime}_{0}+\mathcal{O}((1+B_{z})\varepsilon),

\displaystyle\eta_{x}

\displaystyle=1+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}t[w^{\prime}_{0}+\mathcal{O}((1+B_{z})\varepsilon)],

for all
(x,t)\in\mathbb{T}\times[0,T_{*}\wedge\frac{2}{1+\alpha}(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}))
.
Since
\eta_{x}>0
for all
t<T_{*}
, it follows from (
3.6b
) that

\displaystyle 0<\eta_{x}<1-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}t[(1-\varepsilon)+\mathcal{O}((1+B_{z})\varepsilon)]

for all
(x,t)\in\mathbb{T}\times[0,T_{*}\wedge\frac{2}{1+\alpha}(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}))
. Therefore,

\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}T_{*}\wedge(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\leq\tfrac{1}{1-\varepsilon+\mathcal{O}((1+B_{z})\varepsilon)}=1+\mathcal{O}((1+B_{z})\varepsilon).

Since
(1+B_{z})^{2}\varepsilon\ll 1
, we can conclude that

\displaystyle T_{*}\leq\tfrac{2}{1+\alpha}[1+\mathcal{O}((1+B_{z})\varepsilon)]<\tfrac{2}{1+\alpha}(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}).

Therefore,
T_{*}=T_{*}\wedge\frac{2}{1+\alpha}(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})
, and the inequalities (
4.7
) are true on
\mathbb{T}\times[0,T_{*})
.

It follows that

\displaystyle\int^{T_{*}}_{0}\Bigl(\|\partial_{y}z(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}k(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\Bigr)dt

\displaystyle\leq T_{*}[B_{z}+(1+\tfrac{3}{2\gamma})B_{k}]\varepsilon,

and

\displaystyle\int^{T_{*}}_{0}\|\partial_{y}w(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\>dt

\displaystyle\leq\int^{T_{*}}_{0}\tfrac{4}{3}\tfrac{1}{\min_{x\in\mathbb{T}}\eta_{x}(x,t)}+\tfrac{3}{2\gamma}B_{k}\varepsilon\>dt.

Since
T_{*}
is finite, it follows from (
3.9
) that

\lim_{t\rightarrow T_{*}}\min_{x\in\mathbb{T}}\eta_{x}(x,t)=0.

For all
t<T_{*}
, our initial data assumption (
3.6b
) implies that

\displaystyle\eta_{x}

\displaystyle>1-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}t[1+\varepsilon+\mathcal{O}((1+B_{z})\varepsilon)].

Sending
t\rightarrow T_{*}
and letting
(1+B_{z})^{2}\varepsilon
be sufficiently small gives us

\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}T_{*}\geq\tfrac{1}{1+\mathcal{O}((1+B_{z})\varepsilon)}=1+\mathcal{O}((1+B_{z})\varepsilon)>1-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}.

∎

5.
Higher order estimates

In this section, we will prove estimates on the derivatives of
\Sigma,\eta_{x},\eta_{x}\mathring{W},\mathring{Z},
and
\mathring{K}
with constants that do not depend on our choice of
(w_{0},z_{0},k_{0})\in\mathcal{A}_{n}(\varepsilon,C_{0})
, and whose dependence on
\varepsilon,\alpha,n,
and
C_{0}
is explicit. For the entirety of this section, we assume that
\varepsilon
is chosen small enough such that the results of the previous section hold.

5.1.
Estimates at time zero

Proposition 5.1
.

Let
\mathcal{C}_{x}
and
\mathcal{C}_{t}
and be constants satisfying

•

\mathcal{C}_{x}\geq 2e^{3}C_{0}
,

•

\mathcal{C}_{x}\gg 1
,

•

\mathcal{C}_{t}\gg(1+\alpha)
,

•

\alpha\mathcal{C}_{x}\ll\mathcal{C}_{t}
.

Then if
(1+\alpha)\varepsilon\ll 1
the following inequalities hold at time
t=0
for all
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
:

(5.1a)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon

(5.1b)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})\varepsilon

(5.1c)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\Sigma_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 7\alpha\varepsilon

(5.1d)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\eta_{xt}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}(1+2\varepsilon)

(5.1e)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 8\tfrac{\alpha}{\gamma}\varepsilon.

Proof.

We will prove this via induction on
\beta_{t}
.

Base Case:
At time
t=0

\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!\mathcal{C}^{m}_{x}}=\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\partial^{m+1}_{x}k_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!\mathcal{C}^{m}_{x}}\leq\big(\tfrac{e^{2}C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{m}\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon.

Since

\displaystyle\partial^{m}_{x}\mathring{Z}=\partial^{m+1}_{x}z_{0}+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\sigma_{0}\partial^{m+1}_{x}k_{0}+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\sum^{m-1}_{j=0}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j+1}_{x}k_{0}\partial^{m-1-j+1}_{x}\sigma_{0},

at time zero, our assumptions
C_{0}\geq 3
and
\mathcal{C}_{x}\geq 2e^{3}C_{0}
imply that

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!\mathcal{C}^{m}_{x}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\big(\tfrac{e^{2}C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{m}(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}(\tfrac{1}{2}+\varepsilon)\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big(\tfrac{C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{m-1}(m+1)^{2}\sum^{m-1}_{j=0}{m\choose{j}}\tfrac{j!(m-1-j)!}{m!}

\displaystyle\leq\big(\tfrac{e^{2}C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{m}(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}(\tfrac{1}{2}+\varepsilon)\tfrac{1}{C_{0}}\big(\tfrac{e^{2}C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{m}\sum^{m-1}_{j=0}\tfrac{1}{m-j}

\displaystyle\leq(\tfrac{1}{2e})^{m}(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}(\tfrac{1}{2}+\varepsilon)\mathbbm{1}_{\{m\geq 1\}}(\tfrac{1}{2e})^{m}(1+\log m)

\displaystyle\leq(\tfrac{1}{2e})^{m}(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}(\tfrac{1}{2}+\varepsilon)\tfrac{1}{2e}\mathbbm{1}_{\{m\geq 1\}}\leq 1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma}.

Similar computations applied to
\eta_{x}\mathring{W}
at time zero also give us the bound

(5.2)

\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\partial^{m}_{x}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!\mathcal{C}^{m}_{x}}\leq 1+(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})\varepsilon<1+2\varepsilon

for all
m=0,\ldots,2n+1
.

Since
\Sigma_{t}=-\alpha\Sigma\partial_{y}z\circ\eta
, at time zero we have

\displaystyle\Sigma_{t}=-\alpha\sigma_{0}z^{\prime}_{0}.

Therefore

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial^{m}_{x}\Sigma_{t}}{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{0}\partial^{m+1}_{x}z_{0}+\sum^{m-1}_{j=0}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j+1}_{x}z_{0}\partial^{m-1-j+1}_{x}\sigma_{0},

and computations analogous to those applied to
\partial^{m}_{x}\mathring{Z}
and
\partial^{m}_{x}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})
give us

\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\Sigma_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!\mathcal{C}^{m}_{x}\alpha\varepsilon}\leq 2(\tfrac{1}{2e})^{m}+(\tfrac{1}{2}+\varepsilon)\tfrac{1}{2e}\mathbbm{1}_{\{m\geq 1\}}\leq 2.

Let
0\leq m\leq 2n+1
. At time zero

\displaystyle(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\sigma_{0}k^{\prime}_{0}(w^{\prime}_{0}+z^{\prime}_{0}),

\displaystyle\tfrac{4\gamma}{\alpha}\partial^{m}_{x}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{0}\sum^{m}_{j=0}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j+1}_{x}k_{0}\partial^{m-j+1}_{x}(w_{0}+z_{0})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\partial^{j_{1}-1+1}_{x}\sigma_{0}\partial^{j_{2}+1}_{x}k_{0}\partial^{j_{3}+1}_{x}(w_{0}+z_{0}).

\displaystyle\implies\tfrac{4\gamma}{\alpha}\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\partial^{m}_{x}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!\mathcal{C}^{m}_{x}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq 2(1+2\varepsilon)\big(\tfrac{C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{m}(m+1)^{3}+\tfrac{(1+2\varepsilon)^{2}}{2}\tfrac{1}{C_{0}}\big(\tfrac{C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{m}(m+1)^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{1}{j_{1}}.

Since

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{1}{j_{1}}=(m+1)\sum^{m}_{j=1}\tfrac{1}{j}-m,

our hypothesis that
\mathcal{C}_{x}\geq 2e^{3}C_{0}
and
C_{0}\geq 3
now gives us

\displaystyle\tfrac{4\gamma}{\alpha}\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\partial^{m}_{x}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!\mathcal{C}^{m}_{x}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq 2(1+2\varepsilon)\big(\tfrac{e^{3}C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{m}+\tfrac{(1+2\varepsilon)^{2}}{2}\tfrac{1}{C_{0}}\big(\tfrac{e^{3}C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{m}\sum^{m}_{j=1}\tfrac{1}{j}

\displaystyle\leq 2(1+2\varepsilon)(\tfrac{1}{2})^{m}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{m\geq 1\}}\tfrac{(1+2\varepsilon)^{2}}{4}\tfrac{1}{C_{0}}\leq 3.

Lastly, since
\eta_{xt}=\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}w^{\prime}_{0}+\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}z_{0}^{\prime}
at time zero, we compute that

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\partial^{m}_{x}\eta_{xt}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!\mathcal{C}^{m}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq\big(\tfrac{e^{2}C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{m}(\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}(1+\varepsilon)+\tfrac{|1-\alpha|}{2}\varepsilon)

\displaystyle=(\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}+\max(1,\alpha)\varepsilon)\big(\tfrac{e^{2}C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{m}\leq\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}(1+2\varepsilon)(\tfrac{1}{2e})^{m}

for all
m=0,\ldots,2n+1
. This concludes the base case.

Inductive Step:
Now fix
0\leq m\leq 2n
and suppose our result is true for all multi indices
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{t}\leq m
.

Let
\beta
have
|\beta|\leq 2n
and
\beta_{t}=m
. Taking
\partial^{\beta}
of (
4.3
), setting
t=0
, and simplifying using the fact that

(5.3)

\partial^{i}_{x}\eta_{x}(x,0)=\delta_{i0}

for all nonnegative integers
i
, we arrive at the identity

\displaystyle\partial^{\beta+e_{t}}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{e_{t}\leq\gamma\leq\beta}{\beta\choose{\gamma}}\partial^{\gamma-e_{t}}\eta_{xt}\partial^{\beta+e_{t}-\gamma}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha\sum_{e_{t}\leq\gamma\leq\beta}{\beta\choose{\gamma}}\partial^{\gamma-e_{t}}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{\beta+e_{x}-\gamma}\mathring{K}+\alpha\sigma_{0}\partial^{\beta+e_{x}}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha\sum^{\beta_{x}}_{j=1}{\beta_{x}\choose{j}}\partial^{j}_{x}\sigma_{0}\partial^{\beta-(j-1)e_{x}}\mathring{K}-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum^{\beta_{x}}_{j=0}{\beta_{x}\choose{j}}\partial^{j}_{x}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\partial^{\beta-je_{x}}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{e_{t}\leq\gamma\leq\beta}{\beta\choose{\gamma}}\partial^{\beta-\gamma}\mathring{K}\partial^{\gamma-e_{t}}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{0\leq\gamma\leq\beta}{\beta\choose{\gamma}}\partial^{\gamma}\mathring{K}\partial^{\beta-\gamma}\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}+\gamma_{3}=\beta\\
\gamma_{1}\geq e_{t}\end{subarray}}{\beta\choose{\gamma_{1}\gamma_{2}\gamma_{3}}}\partial^{\gamma_{1}-e_{t}}\eta_{xt}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\mathring{K}\partial^{\gamma_{3}}\mathring{Z}.

Applying our inductive hypotheses, the inequality (
5.2
), Lemma
B.2
, and (
B.2
)–(
B.3
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta+e_{t}|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta+e_{t}}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta+e_{t}|!\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}+1}_{t}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}(\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}(1+2\varepsilon)+2\alpha^{2}\tfrac{\mathcal{C}_{x}}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}\varepsilon)\sum^{|\beta|}_{j=1}{|\beta|\choose{j}}\tfrac{(|\beta|+2)^{2}(j-1)!(|\beta|+1-j)!}{(|\beta|+1)!j^{2}(|\beta|+2-j)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+2\alpha\tfrac{\mathcal{C}_{x}}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}+\alpha(\tfrac{1}{2}+\varepsilon)\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}\sum^{\beta_{x}}_{j=1}{\beta_{x}\choose{j}}\tfrac{(|\beta|+2)^{2}(j-1)!(|\beta|+1-j)!}{(|\beta|+1)!j^{2}(|\beta|+2-j)^{2}}\big(\tfrac{C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{j-1}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{2}(1+2\varepsilon)\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}\sum^{\beta_{x}}_{j=0}{\beta_{x}\choose{j}}\tfrac{(|\beta|+2)^{2}j!(|\beta|-j)!}{(|\beta|+1)!(j+1)^{2}(|\beta|+1-j)^{2}}\big(\tfrac{C_{0}}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}\big)^{j}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{2\mathcal{C}^{2}_{t}}\tfrac{3\alpha}{4\gamma}\varepsilon\sum^{|\beta|}_{j=1}{|\beta|\choose{j}}\tfrac{(|\beta|+2)^{2}(|\beta|-j)!(j-1)!}{(|\beta|+1)!(|\beta|+1-j)^{2}j^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{2\mathcal{C}_{t}}(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})\varepsilon\sum^{|\beta|}_{j=0}{|\beta|\choose{j}}\tfrac{(|\beta|+2)^{2}j!(|\beta|-j)!}{(|\beta|+1)!(j+1)^{2}(|\beta|+1-j)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{4}(1+2\varepsilon)(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})\varepsilon\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}^{2}_{t}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=|\beta|\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{|\beta|\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\tfrac{(|\beta|+2)^{2}(j_{1}-1)!j_{2}!j_{3}!}{(|\beta|+1)!j_{1}^{2}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}(\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}(1+2\varepsilon)+2\alpha^{2}\tfrac{\mathcal{C}_{x}}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}\varepsilon)\sum^{|\beta|}_{j=1}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{j^{3}(|\beta|+2-j)}+2\alpha\tfrac{\mathcal{C}_{x}}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}+\alpha(1+2\varepsilon)\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}\sum^{|\beta|}_{j=1}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{j^{3}(|\beta|+2-j)}

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\varepsilon)\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}\sum^{|\beta|}_{j=0}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{(j+1)^{2}(|\beta|+1-j)^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}^{2}_{t}}\tfrac{3\alpha}{4\gamma}\varepsilon\sum^{|\beta|}_{j=1}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{j^{3}(|\beta|+1-j)^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})\varepsilon\sum^{|\beta|}_{j=0}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{(j+1)^{2}(|\beta|+1-j)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}(1+2\varepsilon)(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})\varepsilon\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}^{2}_{t}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=|\beta|\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{j_{1}^{3}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}[\alpha\mathcal{C}_{x}(1+\alpha\varepsilon)+(1+\alpha)(1+\varepsilon)(1+\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{t}})].

It follows that the bound (
5.1a
) holds for
\partial^{\beta+e_{t}}\mathring{K}
provided that
\mathcal{C}_{t}\gg\alpha\mathcal{C}_{x}
,
\mathcal{C}_{t}\gg 1+\alpha
, and
(1+\alpha)\varepsilon\ll 1
.

The inductive step for (
5.1b
) works out in the same way: pick
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n
and
\beta_{t}=m
; take
\partial^{\beta}
of (
4.4
); set
t=0
and simplify; carry out the same type of computations as done above; if
\mathcal{C}_{t}
is large enough with respect to
\alpha\mathcal{C}_{x}
and
1+\alpha
, then the right hand side of the inequalities can be made smaller than
1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma}
provided that
(1+\alpha)\varepsilon\ll 1
.

The inductive steps for proving (
5.1c
)–(
5.1e
) will now utilize the fact that we have already proven (
5.1a
) and (
5.1b
) for
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{t}\leq m+1
. Pick
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{t}=m+1
. Note that since
\beta_{t}\geq 1
, we must have
\beta_{x}\leq 2n
. Taking
\partial^{\beta}
of (
4.2a
), setting
t=0
, and simplifying yields

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\alpha}\partial^{\beta}\Sigma_{t}

\displaystyle=-\sigma_{0}\partial^{\beta}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\sigma^{2}_{0}\partial^{\beta}\mathring{K}+\sum^{\beta_{x}}_{j=1}{\beta_{x}\choose{j}}[-\partial^{j-1+1}_{x}\sigma_{0}\partial^{\beta-je_{x}}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{1}{\gamma}\sigma_{0}\partial^{j-1+1}_{x}\sigma_{0}\partial^{\beta-je_{x}}\mathring{K}]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{e_{t}\leq\gamma\leq\beta}{\beta\choose{\gamma}}[-\partial^{\gamma-e_{t}}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{\beta-\gamma}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{1}{\gamma}\sigma_{0}\partial^{\gamma-e_{t}}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{\beta-\gamma}\mathring{K}]

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=\beta_{x}\\
j_{1},j_{2}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{\beta_{x}\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\partial^{j_{1}-1+1}_{x}\sigma_{0}\partial^{j_{2}-1+1}_{x}\sigma_{0}\partial^{\beta_{t}e_{t}+j_{3}e_{x}}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}je_{x}+\gamma\leq\beta\\
\gamma\geq e_{t}\\
j\geq 1\end{subarray}}{\beta\choose{je_{x}\>\gamma\>\>\beta-je_{x}-\gamma}}\partial^{j-1+1}_{x}\sigma_{0}\partial^{\gamma-e_{t}}_{t}\Sigma\partial^{\beta-\gamma-je_{x}}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}+\gamma_{3}=\beta\\
\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}\geq e_{t}\end{subarray}}{\beta\choose{\gamma_{1}\gamma_{2}\gamma_{3}}}\partial^{\gamma_{1}-e_{t}}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{\gamma_{2}-e_{t}}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{\gamma_{3}}\mathring{K}.

Therefore, applying our inductive hypotheses, the fact that (
5.1a
) and (
5.1b
) have been proven for multiindices with
\beta_{t}\leq m+1
, and the inequalities in §
B
gives us

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\Sigma_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}\alpha\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq 2+\tfrac{4}{\gamma}+\mathcal{O}(\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}+\tfrac{\alpha\varepsilon}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}+\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}^{2}_{x}}+\tfrac{\alpha\varepsilon}{\mathcal{C}_{x}\mathcal{C}_{t}}+\tfrac{\alpha^{2}\varepsilon^{2}}{\mathcal{C}^{2}_{t}}).

Since
\mathcal{C}_{x}\gg 1,\mathcal{C}_{t}\gg(1+\alpha),
and
\varepsilon\ll 1
, we have proven (
5.1c
) for our choice of
\beta
.

The proof for (
5.1d
) is analogous: if
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{t}=m+1
, then taking
\partial^{\beta}
of (
4.2d
), setting
t=0
, and simplifying produces

\displaystyle\partial^{\beta}\eta_{xt}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\partial^{\beta}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\sigma_{0}\partial^{\beta}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial^{\beta-e_{t}}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}+\sum_{e_{t}\leq\gamma\leq\beta}{\beta\choose{\gamma}}\big[\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\partial^{\gamma-e_{t}}\eta_{xt}\partial^{\beta-\gamma}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\sigma_{0}\partial^{\gamma-e_{t}}\eta_{xt}\partial^{\beta-\gamma}\mathring{K}\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\sum^{\beta_{x}}_{j=1}{\beta_{x}\choose{j}}\partial^{j-1+1}_{x}\sigma_{0}\partial^{\beta-je_{x}}\mathring{K}+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma_{1}+je_{x}+\gamma_{3}=\beta\\
\gamma_{1}\geq e_{t},j\geq 1\end{subarray}}{\beta\choose{\gamma_{1}je_{x}\gamma_{3}}}\partial^{\gamma_{1}-e_{t}}\eta_{xt}\partial^{j-1+1}_{x}\sigma_{0}\partial^{\gamma_{3}}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}+\gamma_{3}=\beta\\
\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}\geq e_{t},\end{subarray}}{\beta\choose{\gamma_{1}\gamma_{2}\gamma_{3}}}\partial^{\gamma_{1}-e_{t}}\eta_{xt}\partial^{\gamma_{2}-e_{t}}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{\gamma_{3}}\mathring{K}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\eta_{xt}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}(1+\alpha)\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{1+\alpha}(\tfrac{|1-\alpha|}{2}(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})+\tfrac{\alpha}{\gamma})+\mathcal{O}(\tfrac{1+|1-\alpha|}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}+\tfrac{1}{(1+\alpha)\mathcal{C}_{x}}+\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{x}\mathcal{C}_{t}}+\tfrac{\alpha\varepsilon}{\mathcal{C}^{2}_{t}})

\displaystyle\leq 1+\mathcal{O}(\tfrac{1+|1-\alpha|}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}+\tfrac{1}{(1+\alpha)\mathcal{C}_{x}}+\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{x}\mathcal{C}_{t}}+\tfrac{\alpha\varepsilon}{\mathcal{C}^{2}_{t}}).

Since
\varepsilon\ll 1
,
\mathcal{C}_{x}\gg 1
, and
\mathcal{C}_{t}\gg(1+\alpha)
, this proves (
5.1d
).

The inductive step for (
5.1e
) works out in the same way as the inductive steps for (
5.1c
) and (
5.1d
). If
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{t}=m+1
, then using (
5.2
) in addition to the same types of bounds as in the previous computations gives us

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq 2(1+2\varepsilon)\sum^{|\beta|}_{j=0}\tfrac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}}{(j+1)^{2}(|\beta|+1-j)^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon+\tfrac{(1+\alpha)\varepsilon}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}+\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{x}})

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{8\pi^{2}}{3}(1+2\varepsilon)+\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon+\tfrac{(1+\alpha)\varepsilon}{\mathcal{C}_{t}}+\tfrac{1}{\mathcal{C}_{x}}).

Our hypotheses on the relative sizes of
\mathcal{C}_{t},\mathcal{C}_{x},\varepsilon,
and
\alpha
now imply our desired bound on
\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}
.
∎

5.2.
A Priori estimates for time derivatives

In this section, we will fix
T\in(0,T_{*}]
, a function
C_{t}:[0,T_{*})\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{+}
, and a constant
\delta\geq 0
. Suppose that
A_{k}\geq B_{k}
and
A_{z}\geq B_{z}
are constants such that
A_{z}\geq 10A_{k}
and

(5.4a)

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq A_{k}\varepsilon,

(5.4b)

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq A_{z}\varepsilon,

for all
t\in[0,T],\>0\leq m\leq 2n+1.

Proposition 5.2
.

If

•

(1+\alpha)3^{\delta}\ll C_{t}
for all
t\in[0,T]
, and

•

(1+A_{z})\varepsilon\ll 1
,

then

(5.5a)

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}(\Sigma^{-1})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha A_{z}\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha A_{z}\varepsilon,

(5.5b)

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\eta_{xt}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\alpha)(\tfrac{2}{3}\delta_{0m}+3A_{z}\varepsilon),

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{5}{8}A_{k}\varepsilon,

for all
t\in[0,T],\>0\leq m\leq 2n+1.

Proof.

We prove our result via induction on
m
. The base case,
m=0
, is immediate from (
4.5
), (
4.7
), and the fact that
A_{k}\geq B_{k}
and
A_{z}\geq B_{z}
.

For the inductive step, suppose that our inequalities are true up to
m-1
for some
1\leq m\leq 2n+1
.

Applying
\partial^{m}_{t}
to both sides of (
4.2c
) yields

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\alpha}\partial^{m}_{t}(\Sigma^{-1})_{t}

\displaystyle=\Sigma^{-1}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}-\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}+\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j-1}_{t}(\Sigma^{-1})_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{Z}.

The bounds from §
B
now give us

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}(\Sigma^{-1})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}\alpha\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq 3A_{z}+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}A_{k}+4\alpha A^{2}_{z}\varepsilon\tfrac{3^{\delta}}{C_{t}}\sum^{m}_{j=1}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j^{3}(m+1-j)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq[3+\tfrac{1}{20\gamma}+100A_{z}\varepsilon\tfrac{3^{\delta}\alpha}{C_{t}}]A_{z}

\displaystyle\leq 4A_{z}.

Here the last inequality holds because of our hypotheses
(1+A_{z})\varepsilon\ll 1
and
C_{t}\gg 3^{\delta}(1+\alpha)
.

Taking
\partial^{m}_{t}
of (
4.2a
) gives us

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\alpha}\partial^{m}_{t}\Sigma_{t}

\displaystyle=-\Sigma\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\Sigma^{2}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}-\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{1}{\gamma}\Sigma\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1},j_{2}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\partial^{j_{1}-t}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{j_{2}-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{K}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-m\delta}\partial^{m}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}\alpha\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq 3A_{z}+\tfrac{9}{2\gamma}A_{k}+\tfrac{3^{\delta}4\alpha A_{z}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}(A_{z}+\tfrac{3}{\gamma}A_{k})\sum^{m}_{j=1}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j^{3}(m+1-j)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\big(\tfrac{3^{\delta}4\alpha A_{z}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}\big)^{2}A_{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1},j_{2}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j_{1}^{3}j^{3}_{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 3A_{z}+\tfrac{9}{2\gamma}A_{k}+25\tfrac{3^{\delta}4\alpha A_{z}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}(A_{z}+\tfrac{3}{\gamma}A_{k})+66\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\big(\tfrac{3^{\delta}4\alpha A_{z}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}\big)^{2}A_{k}

\displaystyle\leq 4A_{z}.

Taking
\partial^{m}_{t}
of (
4.2d
) gives us

\displaystyle\partial^{m}_{t}\eta_{xt}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\eta_{x}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\eta_{x}\Sigma\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial^{m-1}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}+\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}m\eta_{xt}\partial^{m-1}_{t}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}m\eta_{xt}\Sigma\partial^{m-1}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\sum^{m}_{j=2}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\sum^{m}_{j=2}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\Sigma\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\eta_{x}\partial^{j-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1},j_{2}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\partial^{j_{1}-t}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{j_{2}-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{K}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-m\delta}\partial^{m}_{t}\eta_{xt}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{3|1-\alpha|}{2}A_{z}+\tfrac{9\alpha}{2\gamma}A_{k}+\tfrac{(1+\alpha)3^{\delta}}{C_{t}}(\tfrac{m+1}{m})^{2}\bigg[\tfrac{1}{2m}\tfrac{5}{8}A_{k}+\tfrac{|1-\alpha|}{2}A_{z}+\tfrac{3\alpha}{2\gamma}A_{k}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{3^{\delta}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}\bigg[\tfrac{3|1-\alpha|}{2}(1+\alpha)A_{z}^{2}+\tfrac{9\alpha}{2\gamma}(1+\alpha)A_{k}A_{z}+6\tfrac{\alpha^{2}}{\gamma}A_{k}A_{z}\bigg]\sum^{m}_{j=1}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j^{3}(m+1-j)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{3}{2\gamma}\big(\tfrac{3^{\delta}\alpha\varepsilon}{C_{t}}\big)^{2}(1+\alpha)A_{k}A^{2}_{z}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1},j_{2}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j_{1}^{3}j^{3}_{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}}.

Applying our hypotheses on
C_{t}
and
\varepsilon
, as well as our bounds from §
B
, we obtain our inequality for
\partial^{m}_{t}\eta_{xt}
.

Lastly, taking
\partial^{m}_{t}
of (
4.2e
) gives us

\displaystyle\tfrac{4\gamma}{\alpha}\partial^{m}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}

\displaystyle=\Sigma\eta_{x}\mathring{W}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}+\eta_{x}\Sigma\sum^{m}_{j=0}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}(\Sigma\partial^{j-1}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}+\partial^{j-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}(\eta_{x}\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{Z}+\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\Sigma\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{Z})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1},j_{3}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{3}-1}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=m\\
j_{1},j_{2}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}j_{4}}}\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{j_{2}-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{4}}_{t}\mathring{Z}.

The same types of bounds as before give us

\displaystyle\tfrac{4\gamma}{\alpha}\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}A_{k}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq 4+9A_{z}\varepsilon\sum^{m}_{j=0}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{(j+1)^{2}(m+1-j)^{2}}+\tfrac{3^{\delta}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}(\tfrac{15}{8}A_{k}+\tfrac{16}{3}\alpha A_{z})\sum^{m}_{j=1}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j^{3}(m+1-j)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+2(1+\alpha)\tfrac{3^{\delta}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}A_{z}\sum^{m-1}_{j=0}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{(j+1)^{2}(m-j)^{2}}+3(4\alpha+3(1+\alpha))\tfrac{3^{\delta}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}A^{2}_{z}\varepsilon\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j^{3}_{1}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{5\alpha}{2}A_{k}A_{z}\big(\tfrac{3^{\delta}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}\big)^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1},j_{3}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j^{3}_{1}(j_{2}+1)^{2}j^{3}_{3}}+\tfrac{8}{3}\alpha(1+\alpha)A^{2}_{z}\big(\tfrac{3^{\delta}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}\big)^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=m-1\\
j_{2}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j^{3}_{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}(j_{4}+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+12\alpha(1+\alpha)A^{3}_{z}\varepsilon\big(\tfrac{3^{\delta}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}\big)^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=m\\
j_{1},j_{2}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j^{3}_{1}j^{3}_{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}(j_{4}+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 5.

∎

Corollary 5.3
.

Under the same hypotheses as the previous proposition, we have

(5.6a)

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\eta_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 3,

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{4}{3},

(5.6b)

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\Sigma_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 1,

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}(\Sigma^{-1})_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 10,

for all
t\in[0,T],\>0\leq m\leq 2n+1
and

(5.7)

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m-1}_{t}\partial_{x}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{10}{\alpha}A_{k}\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m-1}_{t}\partial_{x}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{5}{\alpha}A_{z}\varepsilon,

for all
t\in[0,T],\>1\leq m\leq 2n+1
.

Proof.

The bounds (
5.6a
) follow immediately from (
4.7
) in the case where
m=0
, and the
1\leq m\leq 2n+1
case follows immediately from (
5.5b
) and our assumption that
(1+\alpha)3^{\delta}\ll C_{t}
. The bounds on
\partial^{m}_{t}\Sigma_{x}
are proven by taking
\partial^{m}_{t}
of (
4.2b
), applying (
5.5
), and using the bounds from §
B
along with our assumption that
(1+A_{z})\varepsilon\ll 1
. To get our bounds on
\partial^{m}_{t}(\Sigma^{-1})_{x}
, take
\partial^{m}_{t}
of the equation

(5.8)

(\Sigma^{-1})_{x}=-\Sigma^{-2}\Sigma_{x}

and apply (
5.5
) together with our bounds on
\partial^{m}_{t}\Sigma_{x}
.

The estimates (
5.7
) can be obtained by taking
\partial^{m-1}_{t}
of the identities

(5.9a)

\displaystyle\alpha\partial_{x}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle=\Sigma^{-1}\eta_{x}\partial_{t}\mathring{K}+\tfrac{1}{2}\Sigma^{-1}\mathring{K}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+\tfrac{1}{2}\Sigma^{-1}\eta_{x}\mathring{K}\mathring{Z},

(5.9b)

\displaystyle\alpha\partial_{x}\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}\Sigma^{-1}\eta_{x}\partial_{t}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{1-\alpha}{4}\Sigma^{-1}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{8\gamma}\mathring{K}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{4}\Sigma^{-1}\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}^{2}-\tfrac{\alpha}{8\gamma}\eta_{x}\mathring{K}\mathring{Z}.

and then using (
5.5
), our inequalities from §
B
, and our assumption that
(1+A_{z})\varepsilon\ll 1
.
∎

5.3.
Energy estimates for time derivatives

Pick constants
\delta,\kappa
with

(5.10a)

\displaystyle\delta

\displaystyle\geq\tfrac{6}{\min(1,\alpha)},

(5.10b)

\displaystyle\kappa

\displaystyle:=\max(1,\alpha)(2+5\delta)

and define the function
C_{t}:[0,T_{*})\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{+}
,

(5.11)

C_{t}(t):=C_{t}(0)e^{\kappa t}

where
C_{t}(0)
is some positive constant to be determined.

Proposition 5.4
.

Suppose
C_{t}(0)
satisfies

\displaystyle C_{t}(0)

\displaystyle\gg(1+\alpha)3^{\delta},

\displaystyle C_{t}(0)

\displaystyle\gg\max(1,\alpha)2^{\delta}C_{0},

and
\varepsilon
satisfies

(1+\alpha)^{2}(1+B_{z})^{2}\varepsilon\ll 1.

Then

(5.12a)

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq B_{k}\varepsilon,

(5.12b)

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq B_{z}\varepsilon,

for all
t\in[0,T_{*})
and
0\leq m\leq 2n+1
.

Proof.

These estimates will be proven via
L^{q}
energy estimates similar to those performed in the proof of Proposition
4.1
. Fix
T\in[0,T_{*})
and pick a constants
A_{k},A_{z}
satisfying

B_{k}<A_{k}<2B_{k}<B_{z}<A_{z}<2B_{z}.

Our bootstrap hypothesis will be that

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq A_{k}\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq A_{z}\varepsilon,

for all
t\in[0,T],\>0\leq m\leq 2n+1.
Our assumptions on
C_{t}(0)
paired with the fact that
A_{z}<2B_{z}
and
(1+B_{z})\varepsilon\ll 1
imply that the hypotheses of Proposition
5.2
and Corollary
5.3
are satisfied with our choice of
\delta
,
T
,
A_{k}
, and
A_{z}
. Additionally, if we define
\mathcal{C}_{t}:=\frac{1}{2}2^{-\delta}C_{t}(0)
, then our hypotheses on
C_{t}(0)
imply that
\mathcal{C}_{t}\gg\alpha C_{0}
and
\mathcal{C}_{t}\gg 1+\alpha
so that Proposition
5.1
gives us

(5.13)

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2^{m}},

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2^{m-1}},

at time
t=0
. Therefore, our bootstrap hypothesis is true for
T=0
.

For
1<q<\infty
and
1\leq m\leq 2n+1
define the energies

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{m,q}_{k}(t)

\displaystyle:=\int_{\mathbb{T}}\Sigma^{-\delta mq}\eta_{x}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}\>dx,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{m,q}_{z}(t)

\displaystyle:=\int_{\mathbb{T}}\Sigma^{-\delta mq}\eta_{x}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}|^{q}\>dx,

\displaystyle E^{m,q}_{k}(t)

\displaystyle:=\frac{(m+1)^{2q}\mathcal{E}^{m,q}_{k}(t)}{(m!)^{q}C_{t}(t)^{mq}},

\displaystyle E^{m,q}_{z}(t)

\displaystyle:=\frac{(m+1)^{2q}\mathcal{E}^{m,q}_{z}(t)}{(m!)^{q}C_{t}(t)^{mq}}.

Taking
\partial_{t}
of
\mathcal{E}^{m,q}_{k}
yields

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{E}}^{m,q}_{k}

\displaystyle=-\delta mq\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}\tfrac{\Sigma_{t}}{\Sigma}\eta_{x}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}+\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}\eta_{xt}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}

\displaystyle\quad+\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})q\eta_{x}\partial^{m+1}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle=\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}\eta_{x}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}[(\alpha\delta mq+\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2})\mathring{Z}+(-\alpha\delta mq+\alpha)\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\Sigma\mathring{K}]

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})q\eta_{x}\partial^{m+1}_{t}\mathring{K}.

Taking
\partial^{m}_{t}
of (
4.3
) produces the identity

\displaystyle\eta_{x}\partial^{m+1}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle=\alpha\Sigma\partial^{m}_{t}\partial_{x}\mathring{K}-[\tfrac{1}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+\tfrac{1}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}+m\eta_{xt}]\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum^{m}_{j=2}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{m+1-j}_{t}\mathring{K}+\alpha\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\partial_{x}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}\eta_{x}\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j}_{t}\mathring{Z}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j-1}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

(5.14)

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{Z}.

This means that

\displaystyle\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})q\eta_{x}\partial^{m+1}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle=\alpha\int\Sigma^{1-\delta mq}\partial_{x}\big(|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}\big)-q\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}[\tfrac{1}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+\tfrac{1}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}+m\eta_{xt}]

\displaystyle\quad-q\sum^{m}_{j=2}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{m+1-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\partial_{x}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\eta_{x}\partial^{j}_{t}\mathring{Z}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{Z}.

Since

\displaystyle\alpha\int\Sigma^{1-\delta mq}\partial_{x}\big(|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}\big)=\tfrac{\alpha}{2}(\delta mq-1)\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}[\eta_{x}\mathring{W}-\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{1}{\gamma}\eta_{x}\Sigma\mathring{K}],

we get

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{E}}^{m,q}_{k}

\displaystyle=\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}\eta_{x}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}[(\alpha\delta mq+\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2})\mathring{Z}+(-\alpha\delta mq+\alpha)\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\Sigma\mathring{K}]

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+\tfrac{\alpha}{2}(\delta mq-1)\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}[\eta_{x}\mathring{W}-\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{1}{\gamma}\eta_{x}\Sigma\mathring{K}]

\displaystyle\quad-q\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}[\tfrac{1}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+\tfrac{1}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}+m\eta_{xt}]

\displaystyle\quad-q\sum^{m}_{j=2}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{m+1-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\partial_{x}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\eta_{x}\partial^{j}_{t}\mathring{Z}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{Z}.

Simplifying the first four terms on the righthand side of this equation gives us

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{E}}^{m,q}_{k}

\displaystyle=D_{k}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+(D_{k}+\alpha mq)\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}\eta_{x}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}mq\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}\eta_{x}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}\Sigma\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-q\sum^{m}_{j=2}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{m+1-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\partial_{x}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\eta_{x}\partial^{j}_{t}\mathring{Z}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

(5.15)

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{Z},

where

(5.16)

D_{k}=D_{k}(m,q,\alpha):=\tfrac{1}{2}+q[(\tfrac{\alpha}{2}\delta-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2})m-\tfrac{1}{2}].

Our lower bound (
5.10
) and the fact that
q>1
gives us the bounds

(5.17)

\tfrac{1}{2}+\tfrac{3}{2}mq\max(1,\alpha)\leq D_{k}<\tfrac{1}{2}mq\alpha(\delta-1).

Since
D_{k}>0
, the inequality (
4.8
) gives us

(5.18)

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{E}}^{m,q}_{k}+\tfrac{1}{2}D_{k}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q}

\displaystyle\leq 4D_{k}\mathcal{E}^{m,q}_{k}+(B_{z}D_{k}+\alpha mq(B_{z}+\tfrac{3}{2\gamma}B_{k}))\varepsilon\mathcal{E}^{m,q}_{k}

\displaystyle\quad-q\sum^{m}_{j=2}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{m+1-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\partial_{x}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\eta_{x}\partial^{j}_{t}\mathring{Z}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j-1}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}

(5.19)

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1}{2}q\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\int\Sigma^{-\delta mq}|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}|^{q-1}\mathrm{sgn\,}(\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K})\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{Z}.

Because

\dot{E}^{m,q}_{k}=-mq\kappa E^{m,q}_{k}+\tfrac{(m+1)^{2q}\dot{\mathcal{E}}^{m,q}_{k}}{(m!)^{q}C^{mq}_{t}},

multiplying (
5.19
) by
\big(\frac{(m+1)^{2}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\big)^{q}
and using our a priori estimates from §
5.2
gives us

\displaystyle\dot{E}^{m,q}_{k}+\tfrac{1}{2}D_{k}\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q}

\displaystyle\leq[-mq\kappa+(4+B_{z}\varepsilon)D_{k}+\alpha mq(B_{z}+\tfrac{3}{2\gamma}B_{k}))\varepsilon]E^{m,q}_{k}

\displaystyle\quad+q\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q-1}A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{2}\big(\cdots\big),

where

\displaystyle\big(\cdots\big)

\displaystyle\leq(2|1-\alpha|+\tfrac{5\alpha}{\gamma})\sum^{m}_{j=2}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}(m+1-j)}{j^{3}(m+2-j)^{2}}+50\alpha(1+\tfrac{1}{\gamma})\sum^{m}_{j=1}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}(m+1-j)}{j^{3}(m+2-j)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{3}{2}\sum^{m}_{j=1}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{(j+1)^{2}(m+1-j)^{2}}+\tfrac{5\alpha}{8\gamma}\tfrac{3^{\delta}}{C_{t}}\sum^{m}_{j=1}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j^{3}(m+1-j)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{3}\tfrac{(1+\alpha)3^{\delta}}{C_{t}}\sum^{m-1}_{j=0}\tfrac{m+1}{(j+1)^{2}(m-j)^{2}}+(|1-\alpha|+\tfrac{5\alpha}{2\gamma})\tfrac{3^{\delta}A_{z}\varepsilon}{C_{t}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}}{j^{3}_{1}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}}.

Since
(1+\alpha)3^{\delta}\ll C_{t}(0)
,
A_{z}\varepsilon<2B_{z}\varepsilon\ll 1
, and

(5.20)

\sum^{m}_{j=1}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}(m+1-j)}{j^{3}(m+2-j)^{2}}=m+\sum^{m}_{j=2}\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}(m+1-j)}{j^{3}(m+2-j)^{2}}\leq m+m(1+\tfrac{1}{m})\sum^{m}_{j=2}\tfrac{m+1}{j^{3}(m+2-j)}\leq m+10m,

it follows from our bounds in §
B
that

\big(\cdots\big)\leq(1+\alpha)m\mathcal{O}(1)+\mathcal{O}(1).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\dot{E}^{m,q}_{k}+\tfrac{1}{2}D_{k}\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q}

\displaystyle\leq[-mq\kappa+(4+B_{z}\varepsilon)D_{k}+\alpha mq(B_{z}+\tfrac{3}{2\gamma}B_{k}))\varepsilon]E^{m,q}_{k}

\displaystyle\quad+qm\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q-1}\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{2})+q\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q-1}\mathcal{O}(A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{2})

\displaystyle\leq[-mq\kappa+(4+B_{z}\varepsilon)D_{k}+\alpha mq(B_{z}+\tfrac{3}{2\gamma}B_{k}))\varepsilon]E^{m,q}_{k}+m\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}

(5.21)

\displaystyle\quad+(m+1)(q-1)\varepsilon^{\frac{q}{2(q-1)}}\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q}+\mathcal{O}(A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}.

Since
\varepsilon<1
and
\frac{q}{q-1}>1
, this can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\dot{E}^{m,q}_{k}+\bigg[\tfrac{1}{2}D_{k}-(m+1)(q-1)\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg]\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q}

\displaystyle\leq[-mq\kappa+(4+B_{z}\varepsilon)D_{k}+\alpha mq(B_{z}+\tfrac{3}{2\gamma}B_{k}))\varepsilon]E^{m,q}_{k}+m\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}+\mathcal{O}(A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}.

It now follows from the lower and upper bounds in (
5.17
) that

(5.22)

\dot{E}^{m,q}_{k}\leq-mq\max(1,\alpha)E^{m,q}_{k}+m\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}+\mathcal{O}(A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}.

This implies (see Lemma
B.1
) that for all
t\in[0,T]
we have

\displaystyle E^{m,q}_{k}(t)

\displaystyle\leq\max\bigg(E^{m,q}_{k}(0),\frac{\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}}{q\max(1,\alpha)}+\frac{\mathcal{O}(A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}}{mq\max(1,\alpha)}\bigg),

\displaystyle\implies E^{m,q}_{k}(t)^{1/q}

\displaystyle\leq\max\bigg(E^{m,q}_{k}(0)^{1/q},\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)^{1-\frac{1}{q}}A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})+\mathcal{O}(A_{k}A_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})\bigg).

Therefore, (
5.13
) and the fact that
A_{k}<2B_{k},A_{z}<2B_{z},
now gives us

\displaystyle E^{m,q}_{k}(t)^{1/q}

\displaystyle\leq\max\big(\tfrac{1}{2^{m}B_{k}},\mathcal{O}(B_{z}(1+\alpha)\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\big)B_{k}\varepsilon

\displaystyle\forall\>t\in[0,T].

Sending
q\rightarrow\infty
now yields

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\max\big(\tfrac{1}{2^{m}B_{k}},\mathcal{O}(B_{z}(1+\alpha)\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\big)B_{k}\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\forall\>t\in[0,T].

Since
(1+\alpha)^{2}B^{2}_{z}\varepsilon\ll 1
,
m\geq 1
, and
B_{k}>1
, (
5.12a
) now follows.

To obtain the bound (
5.12b
) for
t\in[0,T]
and thus conclude our bootstrap argument, it is best to bound the sum
E^{m,q}_{z}+E^{m,q}_{k}
instead of dealing with
E^{m,q}_{z}
on its own. The proof for bounding
E^{m,q}_{z}+E^{m,q}_{k}
, however, is essentially the same as the computations that were just carried out to bound
E^{m,k}_{q}
.

Taking
\partial^{m}_{t}
of (
4.4
) yields the identity

\displaystyle\eta_{x}\partial^{m+1}_{t}\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle=2\alpha\Sigma\partial^{m}_{t}\partial_{x}\mathring{Z}-[\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+(1+\alpha)\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}+m\eta_{xt}-\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\eta_{x}\Sigma\mathring{K}]\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}-\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\Sigma\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum^{m}_{j=2}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{m+1-j}_{t}\mathring{Z}+2\alpha\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\partial_{x}\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\eta_{x}\sum^{m-1}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j}_{t}\mathring{Z}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\eta_{x}\Sigma\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\partial^{j}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum^{m}_{j=1}{m\choose{j}}\bigg[\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\partial^{j-1}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\Sigma\partial^{j-1}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\partial^{j-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}\partial^{m-j}_{t}\mathring{K}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\big(-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\Sigma\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{K}\big)\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\eta_{x}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=m\\
j_{1},j_{3}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}}\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{j_{2}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{3}-1}_{t}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}

(5.23)

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=m\\
j_{1},j_{2}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{m\choose{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}j_{4}}}\partial^{j_{1}-1}_{t}\eta_{xt}\partial^{j_{2}-1}_{t}\Sigma_{t}\partial^{j_{3}}_{t}\mathring{K}\partial^{j_{4}}_{t}\mathring{Z}.

Computations analogous to those performed above for
E^{m,q}_{k}
give us the inequality

\displaystyle\dot{E}^{m,q}_{z}+\tfrac{1}{2}D_{z}\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q}

\displaystyle\leq[-mq\kappa+4D_{z}+\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)(mq+1)B_{z}\varepsilon)]E^{m,q}_{z}

\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{\alpha}{\gamma}q\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q-1}\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]

(5.24)

\displaystyle\quad+qm\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q-1}\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)A^{2}_{z}\varepsilon^{2})+q\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q-1}\mathcal{O}(A_{z}^{2}\varepsilon^{2}),

where
D_{z}
is defined as

(5.25)

D_{z}:=\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}+q[(\alpha\delta-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2})m-\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}].

The constant
D_{z}
satisfies the bounds

(5.26)

-\tfrac{1}{2}(q-1)+5mq\max(1,\alpha)\leq D_{z}<mq\alpha\delta.

Adding (
5.21
) and (
5.24
) together gives us

\displaystyle\dot{E}^{m,q}_{k}+\dot{E}^{m,q}_{z}+\bigg[\tfrac{1}{2}D_{k}-\tfrac{\alpha}{\gamma}-(m+1)(q-1)\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg]\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{n}_{t}}\bigg]^{q}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg[\tfrac{1}{2}D_{z}-(q-1)(\tfrac{\alpha}{\gamma}+(m+1)\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})\bigg]\bigg[\tfrac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg]^{q}

\displaystyle\leq[-mq\kappa+4\max(D_{k},D_{z})+\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)(mq+1)B_{z}\varepsilon)]\big(E^{m,q}_{k}+E^{m,q}_{z}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+2m\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)A^{2}_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}+2\mathcal{O}(A^{2}_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}.

Using (
5.17
), (
5.26
) , and (
5.10
) gives us

\displaystyle\dot{E}^{m,q}_{k}+\dot{E}^{m,q}_{z}

\displaystyle\leq-mq\max(1,\alpha)\big(E^{m,q}_{k}+E^{m,q}_{z}\big)+2m\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)A^{2}_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}+2\mathcal{O}(A^{2}_{z}\varepsilon^{3/2})^{q}.

From here, carrying out the same ODE comparison argument as before and then sending
q\rightarrow\infty
gives us

\displaystyle\max\bigg(\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}},\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\Sigma^{-\delta m}\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!C^{m}_{t}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq B_{z}\varepsilon.

∎

5.4.
Bounds on the remaining derivatives

It follows from the definition (
5.10
) of
\kappa
that
C_{t}
satisfies

\displaystyle C_{t}(t)

\displaystyle\leq C_{t}(0)e^{\kappa T_{*}}<C_{t}(0)e^{2(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})(2+5\delta)}

for all
t\in[0,T_{*})
. Therefore, if we define the constant

(5.27)

\displaystyle\overline{C}_{t}

\displaystyle:=C_{t}(0)e^{(11+\log 3)\delta+5},

then

(5.28)

\overline{C}_{t}>3^{\delta}C_{t}(t)

for all
t\in[0,T_{*})
. It follows from Proposition
5.4
and (
4.7
) that

(5.29)

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!\overline{C}^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq B_{k}\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\frac{(m+1)^{2}\|\partial^{m}_{t}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{m!\overline{C}^{m}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq B_{z}\varepsilon,

for all
t\in[0,T_{*}),0\leq m\leq 2n+1
.

Proposition 5.5
.

Suppose the hypotheses of Proposition
5.4
are satisfied, and additionally
\overline{C}_{x}
is a constant satisfying

\displaystyle\overline{C}_{x}

\displaystyle\gg 1,

\displaystyle\alpha\overline{C}_{x}

\displaystyle\gg\overline{C}_{t}.

Then if
(1+\alpha)B_{z}\varepsilon\ll 1
the following bounds hold for all
t\in[0,T_{*})
and
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
:

(5.30a)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}\leq B_{k}\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\mathring{Z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}\leq B_{z}\varepsilon,

(5.30b)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\Sigma_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}\leq 4\alpha B_{z}\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\Sigma^{-1})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}\leq 4\alpha B_{z}\varepsilon,

(5.30c)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\eta_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}\leq 3,

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}\leq\tfrac{4}{3},

(5.30d)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\Sigma_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}\leq 1,

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\Sigma^{-1})_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}\leq 10.

Proof.

We proceed by induction on
\beta_{x}
.

Base Case:
When
\beta_{x}=0
, (
5.30a
) is implied by (
5.29
). The rest of the inequalities (
5.30b
)–(
5.30d
) in the case
\beta_{x}=0
now follow by applying the a priori estimates of §
5.2
with
A_{k}=B_{k},A_{z}=B_{z},\delta=0
, and
C_{t}=\overline{C}_{t}
.

Inductive Step:
Fix
m
with
0\leq m\leq 2n
and suppose that the estimates (
5.30a
) - (
5.30d
) are true for choices of
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{x}\leq m
.

We first prove that (
5.30a
) is true for choices of
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{x}=m+1
. This needs to be done first, because it will be used to prove that the rest of the bounds (
5.30b
) - (
5.30d
) hold for
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{x}=m+1
.

Fix a choice of
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n
and
\beta_{x}=m
. We will prove that (
5.30a
) is true for
\beta+e_{x}
. To do this, take
\partial^{\beta}
of (
5.9a
) to obtain

\displaystyle\alpha\partial^{\beta+e_{x}}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle=\Sigma^{-1}\eta_{x}\partial^{\beta+e_{t}}\mathring{K}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}je_{x}+\gamma_{2}+\gamma_{3}=\beta\\
j\geq 1\end{subarray}}{\beta\choose je_{x}\gamma_{2}\gamma_{3}}\partial^{j-1}_{x}(\Sigma^{-1})_{x}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\eta_{x}\partial^{\gamma_{3}+e_{t}}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle\hskip-42.67912pt+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}+\gamma_{3}=\beta\\
\gamma_{1}\geq e_{t}\end{subarray}}{\beta\choose\gamma_{1}\gamma_{2}\gamma_{3}}\partial^{\gamma_{1}-e_{t}}(\Sigma^{-1})_{t}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\eta_{x}\partial^{\gamma_{3}+e_{t}}\mathring{K}+\tfrac{1}{2}\Sigma^{-1}\sum_{\gamma\leq\beta}{\beta\choose\gamma}\partial^{\gamma}\mathring{K}\partial^{\beta-\gamma}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})

\displaystyle\hskip-42.67912pt+\tfrac{1}{2}\Sigma^{-1}\hskip-14.22636pt\sum_{\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}+\gamma_{3}=\beta}{\beta\choose\gamma_{1}\gamma_{2}\gamma_{3}}\partial^{\gamma_{1}}\eta_{x}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\mathring{K}\partial^{\gamma_{3}}\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle\hskip-42.67912pt+\tfrac{1}{2}\hskip-17.07164pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}je_{x}+\gamma_{2}+\gamma_{3}=\beta\\
j\geq 1\end{subarray}}{\beta\choose je_{x}\gamma_{2}\gamma_{3}}\partial^{j-1}_{x}(\Sigma^{-1})_{x}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\mathring{K}\partial^{\gamma_{3}}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\hskip 2.84526pt+\hskip 2.84526pt\tfrac{1}{2}\hskip-25.60747pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}je_{x}+\gamma_{2}+\gamma_{3}+\gamma_{4}=\beta\\
j\geq 1\end{subarray}}{\beta\choose je_{x}\gamma_{2}\gamma_{3}\gamma_{4}}\partial^{j-1}_{x}(\Sigma^{-1})_{x}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\eta_{x}\partial^{\gamma_{3}}\mathring{K}\partial^{\gamma_{4}}\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle\hskip-42.67912pt+\tfrac{1}{2}\hskip-17.07164pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}+\gamma_{3}=\beta\\
\gamma_{1}\geq e_{t}\end{subarray}}{\beta\choose\gamma_{1}\gamma_{2}\gamma_{3}}\partial^{\gamma_{1}-e_{t}}(\Sigma^{-1})_{t}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\mathring{K}\partial^{\gamma_{3}}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\hskip 2.84526pt+\hskip 2.84526pt\tfrac{1}{2}\hskip-25.60747pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}je_{x}+\gamma_{2}+\gamma_{3}+\gamma_{4}=\beta\\
\gamma_{1}\geq e_{t}\end{subarray}}{\beta\choose\gamma_{1}\gamma_{2}\gamma_{3}\gamma_{4}}\partial^{\gamma_{1}-e_{t}}(\Sigma^{-1})_{t}\partial^{\gamma_{2}}\eta_{x}\partial^{\gamma_{3}}\mathring{K}\partial^{\gamma_{4}}\mathring{Z}.

Applying our inductive hypothesis and Lemma
B.2
gives us

\displaystyle\alpha\frac{(|\beta|+e_{x}|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta+e_{x}|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}+1}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}B_{k}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq 9\tfrac{\overline{C}_{t}}{\overline{C}_{x}}+30\tfrac{\overline{C}_{t}}{\overline{C}^{2}_{x}}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{|\beta|+1}\hskip-17.07164pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=|\beta|\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{j^{3}_{1}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip-99.58464pt+\tfrac{1}{\overline{C}_{x}}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{|\beta|+1}\bigg[24\alpha B_{z}\varepsilon\hskip-17.07164pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=|\beta|\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{j^{3}_{1}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+2)}+2\sum^{|\beta|}_{j=0}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{(j+1)^{2}(|\beta|+1-j)^{2}}+\tfrac{9}{2}B_{z}\varepsilon\hskip-14.22636pt\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=|\beta|}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{(j_{1}+1)^{2}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip-99.58464pt+\tfrac{1}{\overline{C}^{2}_{x}}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{|\beta|+1}\bigg[\hskip 5.69054pt\tfrac{20}{3}\hskip-14.22636pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=|\beta|\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{j^{3}_{1}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}}+15B_{z}\varepsilon\hskip-22.76219pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=|\beta|\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{j^{3}_{1}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}(j_{4}+1)^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip-99.58464pt+\tfrac{4\alpha B_{z}\varepsilon}{\overline{C}_{x}\overline{C}_{t}}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{|\beta|+1}\bigg[\hskip 5.69054pt\tfrac{4}{3}\hskip-14.22636pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=|\beta|\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{j^{3}_{1}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}}+3B_{z}\varepsilon\hskip-22.76219pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}+j_{4}=|\beta|\\
j_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\tfrac{|\beta|+2}{j^{3}_{1}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}(j_{4}+1)^{2}}\bigg].

The computations from §
B
now imply that

\frac{(|\beta|+e_{x}|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\mathring{K}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta+e_{x}|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}+1}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}B_{k}\varepsilon}\lesssim\tfrac{1}{\alpha\overline{C}_{x}}[\overline{C}_{t}+\tfrac{\overline{C}_{t}}{\overline{C}_{x}}+1+(1+\alpha)B_{z}\varepsilon+\tfrac{1+B_{z}\varepsilon}{\overline{C}_{x}}+\tfrac{\alpha B_{z}\varepsilon(1+B_{z}\varepsilon)}{\overline{C}_{t}}].

If
\alpha\overline{C}_{x}\gg\overline{C}_{t}
and
\overline{C}_{x}\gg 1
, then the righthand side is less than 1, which proves (
5.30a
) for
\partial^{\beta+e_{x}}\mathring{K}
. The bound on
\partial^{\beta+e_{x}}\mathring{Z}
can be proven in an identical manner, so the inductive step for (
5.30a
) is complete.

We next prove (
5.30b
) is true for
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{x}=m+1
. Pick
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{x}=m+1
, and take
\partial^{\beta}
of (
4.2a
). Using the the inductive hypothesis and the fact that (
5.30a
) has been proven already for multi-indices with
\beta_{x}\leq m+1
, our hypotheses on
\overline{C}_{t},\overline{C}_{x}
and
\varepsilon
imply that

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\Sigma_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}\alpha B_{z}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq 3+\tfrac{9}{2\gamma}\tfrac{B_{k}}{B_{z}}+\mathcal{O}(\tfrac{\alpha B_{z}\varepsilon}{\overline{C}_{t}}+\tfrac{1}{\overline{C}_{x}})<4,

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\Sigma^{-1})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}\alpha B_{z}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq 3+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\tfrac{B_{k}}{B_{z}}+\mathcal{O}(\tfrac{\alpha B_{z}\varepsilon}{\overline{C}_{t}}+\tfrac{1}{\overline{C}_{x}})<4.

Now we prove the inequalities (
5.30c
) are true for
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{x}=m+1
. We will do this via a bootstrap argument. Fix a time
T\in[0,T_{*})
, and suppose that

\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\eta_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}\leq 4,\qquad\mbox{and}\qquad\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}\leq 2,

for all
t\in[0,T]
and all
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1,\beta_{x}=m+1
and. Taking
\partial^{\beta}
of (
4.2d
) and (
4.2e
) and applying our bootstrap hypotheses, our inductive hypotheses, and the fact that (
5.30a
) has already been proven for multi-indices with
\beta_{x}\leq m+1
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\eta_{xt}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}+\mathcal{O}((1+\alpha)B_{z}\varepsilon)\leq\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}(2+\mathcal{O}(B_{z}\varepsilon)),

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim B_{z}\varepsilon,

for all
t\in[0,T]
and
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1,\beta_{x}=m+1
. At time
t=0
, Proposition
5.1
and (
5.2
) imply that

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{2}

for all
\beta
with
1\leq|\beta|\leq 2n+1
. Now the identities

\displaystyle\partial^{\beta}\eta_{x}

\displaystyle=\int^{t}_{0}\partial^{\beta}\eta_{xt}\>ds,

\displaystyle\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})

\displaystyle=\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})(\cdot,0)+\int^{t}_{0}\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\>ds

together with (
4.12a
) and our hypotheses on
\varepsilon
imply

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\eta_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})(2+\mathcal{O}(B_{z}\varepsilon))<3,

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 1.

This completes our bootstrap argument and proves (
5.30c
) for
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{x}\leq m+1
.

From here, one can prove that (
5.30d
) is true for
\partial^{\beta}\Sigma_{x}
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{x}=m+1
by applying
\partial^{\beta}
to (
4.2b
) and using the inductive hypotheses along with the fact that we have already proven (
5.30a
) for
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{x}=m+1
.

Lastly, our bounds on
\partial^{\beta}(\Sigma^{-1})_{x}
can now be proven by taking
\partial^{\beta}
of the identity (
5.8
) and using the fact that our bounds on
\partial^{\beta}\Sigma_{x}
have been proven for
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
and
\beta_{x}\leq m+1
.
∎

Corollary 5.6
.

Under the hypotheses of the previous proposition, we also have that

(5.31a)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\Sigma\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 3,

(5.31b)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{5}{8}B_{z}\varepsilon,

(5.31c)

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\eta_{xt}-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 20\max(1,\alpha)B_{z}\varepsilon,

for
|\beta|\leq 2n+1
.

Proof.

The inequality (
5.31a
) follows from (
4.7
), (
5.30b
), (
5.30d
), and our hypotheses on
\overline{C}_{t}
and
\overline{C}_{x}
. The inequality (
5.31b
) follows from taking
\partial^{\beta}
of (
4.2e
) and applying the bounds from Proposition
5.5
.

Taking
\partial^{\beta}
of (
4.2d
) and applying (
5.30a
), (
5.30c
), (
5.31a
), and Lemma
B.3
gives us

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\eta_{xt}-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial^{\beta}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!\overline{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\overline{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}B_{z}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{3|1-\alpha|}{2}\sum^{|\beta|}_{j=0}\tfrac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}}{(j+1)^{2}(|\beta|+1-j)^{2}}+\tfrac{9\alpha}{2\gamma}\tfrac{B_{k}}{B_{z}}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}=|\beta|}\tfrac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}}{(j_{1}+1)^{2}(j_{2}+1)^{2}(j_{3}+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{3|1-\alpha|}{2}\cdot\tfrac{4\pi^{2}}{3}+\tfrac{9\alpha}{2\gamma}\cdot\tfrac{1}{3e^{21}}\cdot\tfrac{3\pi^{4}}{4}

\displaystyle=2\pi^{2}|1-\alpha|+\tfrac{9\pi^{4}\alpha}{8(2\alpha+1)e^{21}}

\displaystyle<20\max(1,\alpha).

∎

Corollary 5.7
.

Under the hypotheses of the previous propositions, we have that

(5.32a)

\displaystyle\|\partial^{i}_{x}\eta_{xt}-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial^{i+1}_{x}w_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq 21\max(1,\alpha)\tfrac{i!}{(i+1)^{2}}\overline{C}^{i}_{x}B_{z}\varepsilon

for all
t\in[0,T_{*}),i=0,\ldots,2n+1
,

(5.32b)

\displaystyle\|\partial^{i}_{x}\eta_{x}-(\delta_{i0}+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}t\partial^{i+1}_{x}w_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq 21\max(1,\alpha)t\tfrac{i!}{(i+1)^{2}}\overline{C}^{i}_{x}B_{z}\varepsilon,

for all
t\in[0,T_{*}],i=0,\ldots,2n+1
, and

(5.32c)

\displaystyle\|\partial^{i}_{x}\partial_{t}\eta_{xt}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq 21(1+\alpha)\tfrac{i!}{i+1}\overline{C}^{i}_{x}B_{z}\varepsilon,

for
t\in[0,T_{*}),i=0,\ldots,2n
.

Proof.

Since

\partial^{i}_{x}\eta_{xt}-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial^{i+1}_{x}w_{0}=\partial^{i}_{x}\eta_{xt}-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial^{i}_{x}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\partial^{i}_{x}(\sigma_{0}k^{\prime}_{0})+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\int^{t}_{0}\partial^{i}_{x}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\>ds,

it follows from Corollary
5.6
that for
i=0,\ldots,2n+1
we have

\displaystyle\tfrac{(i+1)^{2}\|\partial^{i}_{x}\eta_{xt}-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial^{i+1}_{x}w_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{i!\overline{C}^{i}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq 20\max(1,\alpha)B_{z}\varepsilon+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{4}\tfrac{(i+1)^{2}\|\partial^{i}_{x}(\sigma_{0}k^{\prime}_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{i!\overline{C}^{i}_{x}}+\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}T_{*}\tfrac{5\alpha}{4\gamma}B_{z}\varepsilon.

Since
\overline{C}_{x}>\overline{C}_{t}>3^{\delta}C_{t}(0)>6^{\delta}C_{0}
, it follows (see the computations in the proof of Proposition
5.1
) that

\tfrac{(i+1)^{2}\|\partial^{i}_{x}(\sigma_{0}k^{\prime}_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{i!\overline{C}^{i}_{x}}<\varepsilon.

Therefore, we arrive at

\displaystyle\tfrac{(i+1)^{2}\|\partial^{i}_{x}\eta_{xt}-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial^{i+1}_{x}w_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{i!\overline{C}^{i}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq\max(1,\alpha)B_{z}\varepsilon[20+\tfrac{1}{2B_{z}}+\tfrac{5}{8}(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}})]<21\max(1,\alpha)B_{z}\varepsilon.

This bound implies (
5.32a
).

To obtain (
5.32b
), integrate (
5.32a
) in time. The last inequality (
5.32c
) is a direct application of (
5.31b
) and (
5.31c
).
∎

6.
Stability estimates with respect to perturbations of
w_{0}

We will now quantify the stability of solutions with initial data in
\mathcal{A}_{n}(\varepsilon,C_{0})
with respect to perturbations in
w_{0}
. Fix a specific choice of data
(\overline{w}_{0},z_{0},k_{0})\in\mathcal{A}_{n}(\varepsilon,C_{0})
. In this section, we will consider initial data
(w_{0},z_{0},k_{0})
of the form

w_{0}=\overline{w}_{0}+\lambda\widetilde{w}_{0}\,,\qquad z_{0}=z_{0}\,,\qquad k_{0}=k_{0}\,,

where
\widetilde{w}_{0}\in W^{2n+2,\infty}(\mathbb{T})
satisfies

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{w}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq L,

\displaystyle\frac{\|\partial^{i+1}_{x}\widetilde{w}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}}{i!}

\displaystyle\leq LC_{0}^{i}

\displaystyle\forall\>i=0,\ldots,2n+1,

for some positive constant
L
, and
\lambda
is in a small enough neighborhood of 0 that
(w_{0},z_{0},k_{0})
is still in
\mathcal{A}_{n}(\varepsilon,C_{0})
for all
\lambda
considered. Such initial data now defines a family of solutions
\mathring{K}=\mathring{K}(x,t,\lambda),\mathring{Z}=\mathring{Z}(x,t,\lambda)
, etc. with initial data in
\mathcal{A}_{n}(\varepsilon,C_{0})
. Our goal is the estimate the partial derivatives
(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{\lambda},\mathring{Z}_{\lambda},\mathring{K}_{\lambda},\Sigma_{\lambda},
etc. and their derivatives in space
x
and time
t
. In the end, we will obtain the estimates stated in §
6.4
, which should be viewed as extensions to the bounds obtained earlier in §
5.4
. These estimates will be used in §
8.4
. The results in this section and their proofs will be essentially analogous to those found in §
5
, with only minor differences. For this reason, less detail will be provided; however, the key differences between the proofs in this section and the corresponding proofs in the previous section will be highlighted.

Taking
\partial_{\lambda}
of (
4.5
) gives us the system

(6.1a)

\displaystyle\partial_{\lambda}\Sigma_{t}

\displaystyle=\alpha\Sigma_{\lambda}(-\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{1}{\gamma}\Sigma\mathring{K})-\alpha\Sigma\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\Sigma^{2}\mathring{K}_{\lambda},

(6.1b)

\displaystyle\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{xt}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{\lambda}+\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}(\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\Sigma\mathring{K})+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\eta_{x}\Sigma_{\lambda}\mathring{K}+\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}+\tfrac{\alpha}{2\gamma}\eta_{x}\Sigma\mathring{K}_{\lambda},

(6.1c)

\displaystyle\partial_{\lambda}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}(\Sigma_{\lambda}\mathring{K}+\Sigma\mathring{K}_{\lambda})(\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+\eta_{x}\mathring{Z})+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\Sigma\mathring{K}[(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{\lambda}+\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}+\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}],

(6.1d)

\displaystyle\partial_{\lambda}\Sigma_{x}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{\lambda}-\tfrac{1}{2}\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}-\tfrac{1}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}[\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\Sigma\mathring{K}+\eta_{x}\Sigma_{\lambda}\mathring{K}+\eta_{x}\Sigma\mathring{K}_{\lambda}],

(6.1e)

\displaystyle\eta_{x}\partial_{t}\mathring{K}_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\alpha\Sigma\partial_{x}\mathring{K}_{\lambda}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathring{K}_{\lambda}[\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}]-\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\partial_{t}\mathring{K}+\alpha\Sigma_{\lambda}\partial_{x}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{K}\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathring{K}[(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{\lambda}+\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}],

(6.1f)

\displaystyle\eta_{x}\partial_{t}\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=2\alpha\Sigma\partial_{x}\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}+\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}[-\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{W}-(1+\alpha)\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\eta_{x}\Sigma\mathring{K}]

\displaystyle-\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\partial_{t}\mathring{Z}+2\alpha\Sigma_{\lambda}\partial_{x}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\mathring{K}_{\lambda}\Sigma(\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}-\eta_{x}\mathring{W})

\displaystyle+\mathring{Z}[-\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{\lambda}-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}(\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\Sigma+\eta_{x}\Sigma_{\lambda})\mathring{K}].

Mulitplying (
6.1e
) and (
6.1f
) by
\Sigma^{-1}
and rearranging gives us

(6.1g)

\displaystyle\alpha\partial_{x}\mathring{K}_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\eta_{x}\Sigma^{-1}\partial_{t}\mathring{K}_{\lambda}+\tfrac{1}{2}\mathring{K}_{\lambda}\Sigma^{-1}[\eta_{x}\mathring{W}+\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}]+\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\Sigma^{-1}\partial_{t}\mathring{K}-\alpha\Sigma_{\lambda}\Sigma^{-1}\partial_{x}\mathring{K}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{2}\eta_{x}\mathring{K}\Sigma^{-1}\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}+\tfrac{1}{2}\Sigma^{-1}\mathring{K}[(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{\lambda}+\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}],

(6.1h)

\displaystyle 2\alpha\partial_{x}\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\eta_{x}\Sigma^{-1}\partial_{t}\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}-\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}[-\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\Sigma^{-1}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})-(1+\alpha)\eta_{x}\Sigma^{-1}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\eta_{x}\mathring{K}]

\displaystyle+\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\Sigma^{-1}\partial_{t}\mathring{Z}-2\alpha\Sigma_{\lambda}\Sigma^{-1}\partial_{x}\mathring{Z}-\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}\mathring{K}_{\lambda}(\eta_{x}\mathring{Z}-\eta_{x}\mathring{W})

\displaystyle-\mathring{Z}[-\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\Sigma^{-1}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{\lambda}-\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}\Sigma^{-1}\mathring{Z}+\tfrac{\alpha}{4\gamma}(\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{x}+\eta_{x}\Sigma_{\lambda}\Sigma^{-1})\mathring{K}].

Notice that
\partial_{\lambda}(\Sigma^{-1})
does not appear in any of the above equations; for this reason, we will not have to estimate
\partial_{\lambda}(\Sigma^{-1})
or any of its derivatives.

6.1.
Estimates at time zero

Proposition 6.1
.

Let
\mathcal{C}_{x}
and
\mathcal{C}_{t}
and be constants satisfying

•

\mathcal{C}_{x}\geq 2e^{3}C_{0}
,

•

\mathcal{C}_{x}\gg 1
,

•

\mathcal{C}_{t}\gg(1+\alpha)
,

•

\alpha\mathcal{C}_{x}\ll\mathcal{C}_{t}
.

Then if
(1+\alpha)\varepsilon\ll 1
we have the following bounds at time
t=0
for all multi indices
\beta
with
|\beta|\leq 2n
:

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\mathring{K}_{\lambda}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!L\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\mathring{Z}_{\lambda}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!L\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{\lambda}\Sigma_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!L\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 7\alpha\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{\lambda}\eta_{xt}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!L\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\alpha),

\displaystyle\frac{(|\beta|+1)^{2}\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{\lambda}(\eta_{x}\mathring{W})_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}}{|\beta|!L\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{x}}_{x}\mathcal{C}^{\beta_{t}}_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq 8\varepsilon.

Proof.

Our proof will be almost identical to the proof of Proposition
5.1
. We will do induction on
\beta_{t}
.

Base Case:
Recall from the proof of Proposition
5.1
that at time
t=0

\displaystyle\Sigma

\displaystyle=\sigma_{0},

\displaystyle\eta_{x}

\displaystyle=1,

\displaystyle\eta_{x}\mathring{W}

\displaystyle=w^{\prime}_{0}-\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\sigma_{0}k^{\prime}_{0},

\displaystyle\mathring{Z}

\displaystyle=z^{\prime}_{0}+\tfrac{1}{2\gamma}\sigma_{0}k^{\prime}_{0},

\displaystyle\mathring{K}

\displaystyle=k^{\prime}_{0},

\displaystyle\Sigma_{t}

\displaystyle=-\alpha\sigma_{0}z^{\prime}_{0}

\displaystyle\eta_{xt}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1+\alpha}{2}w^{\prime}_{0}+\tfrac{1-\alpha}{2}z^{\prime}_{0},
